# NYC What To Do

**Rolling window:** 2026-10-14 through 2026-10-20

## Best date plans

### 2026-10-17 — Open House New York: Grand Army Plaza Arch Tours + Riverdel

**Open House New York: Grand Army Plaza Arch Tours, then a meal.** Vegan cheese shop and cafe at Riverdel. It is a 7-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 10 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Grand+Army+Plaza%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&destination=820+Washington+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.prospectpark.org/event/ohny-soldiers-and-sailors-memorial-arch-tours/) · [Restaurant](https://www.riverdelcheese.com/)

### 2026-10-17 — Wellness For The People Presents: The Festival + Riverdel

**Wellness For The People Presents: The Festival, then a meal.** Vegan cheese shop and cafe at Riverdel. It is a 15-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 8:30 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=101+East+Drive%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11225&destination=820+Washington+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.prospectpark.org/event/wellness-for-the-people-presents-the-festival/) · [Restaurant](https://www.riverdelcheese.com/)

### 2026-10-17 — Brooklyn Navy Yard Annual Open House + Place des Fetes

**Brooklyn Navy Yard Annual Open House, then a meal.** Wine bar, seafood, small plates at Place des Fetes. It is a 17-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 12 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=141+Flushing+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11205&destination=212+Greene+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.brooklynnavyyard.org/events-exhibitions/) · [Event booking](https://bit.ly/3TY7Teo) · [Restaurant](https://www.placedesfetesbk.com/)

### 2026-10-19 — Yellow Rose + Joke Book Comedy

**Dinner, then Joke Book Comedy.** Tex-Mex at Yellow Rose. It is a 14-minute walk from the venue. Joke Book Comedy starts at 8 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=102+3rd+Avenue%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10003&destination=197+E+3rd+St%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10009&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://bookclubbar.com/events/6057520261012) · [Restaurant](https://www.yellowrosetx.com/)

### 2026-10-16 — New York African Restaurant Week Food + Music Festival + Hart's

**New York African Restaurant Week Food + Music Festival, then a meal.** Mediterranean-leaning neighborhood restaurant at Hart's. It is a 12-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 12 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=1368+Fulton+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11216&destination=506+Franklin+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.eventbrite.com/e/2026-new-york-african-restaurant-week-festival--tickets-1986132308199) · [Restaurant](https://www.hartsbrooklyn.com/)

### 2026-10-19 — Autumn Brook Painting Class + Russ & Daughters at Brooklyn Navy Yard

**Autumn Brook Painting Class, then dinner.** Jewish appetizing, bagels, smoked fish at Russ & Daughters at Brooklyn Navy Yard. It is 2.4 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 3 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=329+Greenwich+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10013&destination=141+Flushing+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11205&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.musepaintbar.com/events/classic-painting-345338) · [Restaurant](https://www.russanddaughters.com/)

### 2026-10-18 — New York Mycological Society Fungus Festival + Sawa

**New York Mycological Society Fungus Festival, then a meal.** Lebanese at Sawa. It is 2.6 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=The+Battery%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10004&destination=75+5th+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.newyorkmyc.org/fungus-festival-2026-2/) · [Restaurant](https://www.sawabk.com/)

### 2026-10-16 — Olly Olly Market + Houdini: Preserving a Legacy of Magic, Escapes, and Showmanship

**Dinner, then Houdini: Preserving a Legacy of Magic, Escapes, and Showmanship.** Food hall at Olly Olly Market. It is 1.9 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Doors 6:15 pm — eat first.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=601+West+26th+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10001&destination=40+Lincoln+Center+Plaza+%28entrance+at+111+Amsterdam+Ave+between+W+64th+%26+W+65th+Sts%29&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.nypl.org/events/programs/2026/10/16/houdini-preserving-legacy-magic-escapes-and-showmanship) · [Event booking](https://www.nypl.org/https://qa-www.nypl.org/events/programs/2026/10/16/houdini-preserving-legacy-magic-escapes-and-showmanship) · [Restaurant](https://www.ollyollymarket.com/)

#### [Queens Night Market](https://queensnightmarket.com/)

4:00 PM · markets · Free entry; most dishes capped at a few dollars · Queens Night Market · Corona · Queens · Score 53.4  
Open-air night market behind the New York Hall of Science with about a hundred independent food, art, and merchandise vendors, deliberately kept low-priced.
**Timing:** Saturdays 4pm to midnight. Note the seasonal gap between late August and mid September.
**Practical note:** Outdoors and cash-friendly; the adjacent lot charges for parking, so the 7 train is easier.
**Why now:** Runs every Saturday evening in season; the 7 train to 111th St drops you four blocks away.

A source-checked October dining edit keeps current, restaurant-specific offers and restaurants, trims duplicate heatmap/holiday/opening cards, and removes unsupported pricing or award language.

Dining research: 2026-10-14T10:35:04+00:00

No Michelin star claims are retained. Crown Shy was verified only through its official lunch menu capture, which does not establish Michelin status. Kirbee's source article mentions honors for the pitmasters' Texas restaurants, not a Michelin award for Kirbee's itself.

Schedule: Daily 12pm-7pm  
Conditions: Combo is select one drink and one food item. Reservation link provided; tax, tip, and service charges not stated.

Schedule: Monday-Friday, 12pm-4pm  
Conditions: Select one appetizer, one main, and one side. Some dishes carry supplements. Reservation link provided; tax, tip, and service charges not stated.
